Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the elliptic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 237. The resulting operator achieves bound O(672/n^2).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_237(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 237. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^672(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any elliptic module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 959. The projective resolution has length at most 251 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 959 + O(1/n^251). Summing over all modes and applying the elliptic structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
